e.currentTarget.dataset.target;\n    if (!id) return;\n    const filtered = this.state.fCarrier !== \"all\" || this.state.fService !== \"all\" || this.state.fCategory !== \"all\";\n    const };\n  revealFinding(id) {\n    const el = document.getElementById(id);\n    if (!el) return;\n    const reduce = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia(\"(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)\").matches;\n    // find the nearest scrollable ancestor; fall back to the window\n    let sc = el.parentElement;\n    while (sc && sc !== document.body) {\n      const st = getComputedStyle(sc);\n      if (/(auto|scroll)/.test(st.overflowY) && sc.scrollHeight > sc.clientHeight + 4) break;\n      sc = sc.parentElement;\n    }\n    const inner = sc && sc !== document.body && sc.scrollHeight > sc.clientHeight + 4;\n    const OFF = inner ? 24 : 88;\n    const cur = () => (inner ? sc.scrollTop : window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop);\n    const gap = () => el.getBoundingClientRect().top - (inner ? sc.getBoundingClientRect().top + OFF : OFF);\n    const set = (y) => { const t = Math.max(0, y); if (inner) sc.scrollTop = t; else window.scrollTo(0, t); };\n    const from = cur();\n    const to = Math.max(0, from + gap());\n    cancelAnimationFrame(this._scrollRaf);\n    if (reduce || Math.abs(to - from) < 8) { set(to); return; }\n    // self-driven tween: React commits cancel native smooth scrolling\n    const dur = 300, t0 = performance.now();\n    const ease = (p) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);\n    const step = (now) => {\n      const p = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);\n      set(from + (to - from) * ease(p));\n      if (p < 1) this._scrollRaf = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n      else if (Math.abs(gap()) > 10) set(cur() + gap());\n    };\n    this._scrollRaf = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n    el.classList.remove(\"ia-flash\");\n    void el.offsetWidth;\n    el.classList.add(\"ia-flash\");\n    clearTimeout(this._flashT);\n (isContext ? \"above\" : \"higher\") : diff < 0 ? a.gapScore)[0];\n    scorecardAll.forEach(r => { r.biggestGap = biggest && r.id === biggest.id; });\n    const scoreOpen = !!st.biBenchScoreOpen;\n    const scorecardVisible = scorecardAll.filter(r => [\"cpo\", \"cpu\", \"acc\", \"zone\"].indexOf(r.id) >= 0);\n    const scorecardHidden = scorecardAll.filter(r => [\"upo\", \"scan\"].indexOf(r.id) >= 0);\n    const scorecard = scorecardAll;\n    const biggestGapName = biggest ? biggest.name.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + biggest.name.slice(1) : \"\";\n    const cpoGap = this.r2(you.cpo - K.cpoMed);\n    const gapAnnual = this.biWhole(Math.abs(cpoGap) * (H.orders / (C.isPeriod ? 3 : 1)) * 12);\n    const TIPS = {\n      cpo: \"Measures total logistics spend divided by orders shipped. },\n      \"2026-Q2\": { upo: 1.84, prevUpo: 1.76 },\n      \"2026-04\": { upo: 1.78, prevUpo: 1.74 },\n      \"2026-05\": { upo: 1.79, prevUpo: 1.78 },\n      \"2026-07\": { upo: 1.82, prevUpo: 1.87 Close without saving them?\")) return;\n    this.setState({ outcomeModal: null });\n  };\n  // resolves any currently-open finding (warning + discarding if dirty) before running `next`; returns false if the user cancelled\n  omResolveOpenFinding(om, next) {\n    if (om.openFindingId && this.omFindingDirty(om, om.openFindingId)) {\n      if (!window.confirm(\"You have unsaved changes to this finding. Discard them?\")) return false;\n    }\n    const id = om.openFindingId;\n    const g = id ? (this.state.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id) : null;\n    const drafts = id ? { ...om.drafts, [id]: { ...this.omCommittedDraft(g), error: \"\" } } : om.drafts;\n    next({ ...om, drafts, openFindingId: null });\n    return true;\n  }\n  toggleDispute = (e) => {\n    const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    const isCollapsing = !om.collapsed[key];\n    if (isCollapsing) {\n      const openId = om.openFindingId;\n      const belongsHere = openId && this.disputeKeyFor((this.state.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === openId) || {}) === key;\n      if (belongsHere) {\n        this.omResolveOpenFinding(om, (nextOm) => this.setState({ outcomeModal: { ...nextOm, collapsed: { ...nextOm.collapsed, [key]: true } } }));\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n    this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, collapsed: { ...s.outcomeModal.collapsed, [key]: !s.outcomeModal.collapsed[key] } } }));\n  };\n  toggleOmDropdown = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    if (om.openDropdownId === id) { this.setState({ outcomeModal: { ...om, openDropdownId: null, dropdownPos: null } }); return; }\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const menuWidth = Math.max(rect.width, 220);\n    const menuH = 4 * 42 + 16;\n    const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;\n    const openUp = spaceBelow < menuH + 12 && rect.top > menuH + 12;\n    const top = openUp ? Math.max(8, rect.top - menuH - 6) : rect.bottom + 6;\n    const left = Math.min(Math.max(8, rect.left), window.innerWidth - menuWidth - 8);\n    this.setState({ outcomeModal: { ...om, openDropdownId: id, dropdownPos: { top, left, width: menuWidth } } });\n  };\n  closeOmDropdown = () => this.setState(s => s.outcomeModal ? { outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, openDropdownId: null, dropdownPos: null } } : null);\n  toggleFindingRow = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    if (om.openFindingId === id) { this.omResolveOpenFinding(om, (nextOm) => this.setState({ outcomeModal: nextOm })); return; }\n    this.omResolveOpenFinding(om, (nextOm) => this.setState({ outcomeModal: { ...nextOm, openFindingId: id } }));\n  };\n  cancelFinding = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    this.setState(s => {\n      const g = (s.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id);\n      return { outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, openFindingId: null, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...this.omCommittedDraft(g), error: \"\" } } } };\n    });\n  };\n  selectOmStatus = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id, v = e.currentTarget.dataset.status;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    if (v === \"awaiting\") { this.commitAwaiting(id, om); return; }\n    const apply = (nextOm) => {\n      this.setState(s => {\n        const g = (s.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id);\n        const prev = nextOm.drafts[id] || this.omCommittedDraft(g);\n        let amount = prev.amount, date = prev.date, reason = prev.reason;\n        if (v === \"full\") { amount = String((g && g.amountN) || 0); }\n        else if (v === \"not_issued\") { amount = \"0\"; }\n        else if (v === \"partial\") { amount = prev.status === \"partial\" ? prev.amount : \"\"; }\n        return { outcomeModal: { ...nextOm, openDropdownId: null, openFindingId: id, drafts: { ...nextOm.drafts, [id]: { ...prev, status: v, amount, date, reason, error: \"\", amountEditing: false, autoFullNotice: false } } } };\n      });\n    };\n    if (om.openFindingId && om.openFindingId !== id) { this.omResolveOpenFinding(om, apply); return; }\n    apply(om);\n  };\n  commitAwaiting(id, om) {\n    const g = (this.state.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id); if (!g) return;\n    const hadDetails = !!(g.collection && g.collection.status && g.collection.status !== \"awaiting\");\n    const doCommit = () => {\n      const now = this.nowStr();\n      const history = (g.collection && g.collection.history) ? g.collection.history.slice() : [];\n      if (hadDetails) history.push({ status: g.collection.status, amountN: g.collection.amountN, date: g.collection.date, reason: g.collection.reason, changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now });\n      const next = { status: \"awaiting\", amountN: null, date: null, reason: \"\", changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now, history };\n      this.trackEvent(\"collection_outcome_recorded\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", groupId: id, status: \"awaiting\" });\n      this.setState(s => ({\n        reviewGroups: s.reviewGroups.map(x => x.id === id ? { ...x, collection: next } : x),\n        outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, openDropdownId: null, dropdownPos: null, openFindingId: s.outcomeModal.openFindingId === id ? null : s.outcomeModal.openFindingId, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { status: \"awaiting\", amount: \"\", date: \"\", reason: \"\", error: \"\", amountEditing: false, autoFullNotice: false } } },\n      }));\n    };\n    if (hadDetails && !window.confirm(\"Change to Awaiting outcome? This will remove the saved collection details for this finding.\")) {\n      this.setState({ outcomeModal: { ...om, openDropdownId: null, dropdownPos: null } });\n      return;\n    }\n    doCommit();\n  }\n  onOmAmount = (e) => { const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id, v = e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, \"\"); this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts[id], amount: v, amountEditing: true, autoFullNotice: false, error: \"\" } } } })); };\n  onOmAmountFocus = (e) => { const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts[id], amountEditing: true } } } })); };\n  onOmAmountBlur = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    this.setState(s => {\n      const g = (s.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id);\n      const prev = s.outcomeModal.drafts[id];\n      let next = { ...prev, amountEditing: false };\n      if (prev.status === \"partial\") {\n        const amt = parseFloat(prev.amount || \"\");\n        const pursued = (g && g.amountN) || 0;\n        if (!isNaN(amt) && amt > 0 && amt <= pursued) {\n          if (Math.abs(amt - pursued) < 0.005) next = { ...next, status: \"full\", amount: String(pursued), autoFullNotice: true };\n          else next = { ...next, amount: amt.toFixed(2) };\n        }\n      }\n      return { outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: next } } };\n    });\n  };\n  onOmDate = (e) => { const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id, v = e.target.value; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts[id], date: v, error: \"\" } } } })); };\n  onOmReason = (e) => { const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id, v = e.target.value; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts[id], reason: v } } } })); };\n  openBulk = (e) => { const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, bulk: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk, [key]: { status: null, reason: \"\" } } } })); };\n  cancelBulk = (e) => { const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, bulk: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk, [key]: null } } })); };\n  chooseBulkStatus = (e) => { const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key, status = e.currentTarget.dataset.status; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, bulk: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk, [key]: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk[key], status } } } })); };\n  onBulkReason = (e) => { const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key, v = e.target.value; this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, bulk: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk, [key]: { ...s.outcomeModal.bulk[key], reason: v } } } })); };\n  confirmBulk = (e) => {\n    const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    const bulk = om.bulk[key]; if (!bulk || !bulk.status) return;\n    const status = bulk.status, bulkReason = bulk.reason || \"\";\n    const now = this.nowStr();\n    const targets = (this.state.reviewGroups || []).filter(g => g.pursuit === \"pursued\" && this.disputeKeyFor(g) === key);\n    const updatedGroups = this.state.reviewGroups.map(g => {\n      if (!targets.includes(g)) return g;\n      const history = (g.collection && g.collection.history) ? g.collection.history.slice() : [];\n      if (g.collection && g.collection.status && g.collection.status !== \"awaiting\") history.push({ status: g.collection.status, amountN: g.collection.amountN, date: g.collection.date, reason: g.collection.reason, changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now });\n      const next = { status, amountN: status === \"full\" ? (g.amountN || 0) : (status === \"not_issued\" ? 0 : null), date: status === \"awaiting\" ? null : now, reason: status === \"not_issued\" ? bulkReason : \"\", changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now, history };\n      return { ...g, collection: next };\n    });\n    const newDrafts = { ...om.drafts };\n    targets.forEach(g => { const ug = updatedGroups.find(x => x.id === g.id); newDrafts[g.id] = { ...this.omCommittedDraft(ug), error: \"\" }; });\n    const openFindingId = (om.openFindingId && targets.some(g => g.id === om.openFindingId)) ? null : om.openFindingId;\n    this.trackEvent(\"collection_outcome_bulk_applied\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", key, status, count: targets.length });\n    this.setState({\n      reviewGroups: updatedGroups,\n      outcomeModal: { ...om, bulk: { ...om.bulk, [key]: null }, drafts: newDrafts, openFindingId, collapsed: { ...om.collapsed, [key]: true } },\n    }, () => this.showToast(\"ok\", \"Outcome applied to \" + targets.length + \" finding\" + (targets.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\") + \".\"));\n  };\n  nsAction(kind, tier, label) {\n    const fu = this.state.findingsUnavailable;\n    const handler = kind === \"outcome\" ? (fu ? this.openMemoLevelOutcome : this.openMemoOutcome) : (kind === \"view\" ? (fu ? this.openMemoLevelDetails : this.openDisputeDetails) : this.openPrep);\n    return { kind, tier, label, handler, isPrimary: tier === \"primary\", isSecondary: tier === \"secondary\", isTertiary: tier === \"tertiary\", isPrep: kind === \"prep\", isOutcome: kind === \"outcome\", isView: kind === \"view\" };\n  }\n  nsLine(icon, text) { return { icon, text, isPrep: icon === \"prep\", isClock: icon === \"clock\", isCheck: icon === \"check\" }; }\n  trackerDisputeCta() {\n    if (this.state.findingsUnavailable) return this.memoLevelCta();\n    const groups = this.state.reviewGroups || [];\n    if (!groups.length) return null;\n    const eligible = groups.filter(g => g.pursuit !== \"pursued\" && g.pursuit !== \"excluded\" && !this.groupExpired(g));\n    const pursued = groups.filter(g => g.pursuit === \"pursued\");\n    if (!eligible.length && !pursued.length) return null;\n    const fmtCount = (n) => n + \" finding\" + (n === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\");\n    const verb = (n) => n === 1 ? \"has\" : \"have\";\n    let awaitingCount = 0, finalizedCount = 0;\n    pursued.forEach(g => {\n      const st = g.collection && g.collection.status;\n      if (!st || st === \"awaiting\") awaitingCount++;\n      else finalizedCount++;\n    });\n    const excludedIds = this.state.disputeExcludedIds || [];\n    const excludedCount = eligible.filter(g => excludedIds.includes(g.id)).length;\n    const hasDraft = !pursued.length && excludedCount > 0 && excludedCount < eligible.length;\n\n    if (hasDraft) {\n      return {\n        key: \"draft\", statusLabel: \"DISPUTE DRAFT\", color: \"purple\",\n        supporting: \"Started \" + (this.state.disputeDraftDate || this.todayShort()) + \" \\u00b7 Not yet sent.\",\n        primaryKind: \"draft\", primaryLabel: \"Continue preparing dispute\",\n      };\n    }\n    if (eligible.length && pursued.length) {\n      return {\n        key: \"more\", statusLabel: \"MORE FINDINGS AVAILABLE\", color: \"purple\",\n        supporting: fmtCount(eligible.length) + \" \" + verb(eligible.length) + \" not been submitted.\",\n        primaryKind: \"prep\", primaryLabel: \"Prepare another dispute\",\n        contextual: awaitingCount > 0 ? { text: fmtCount(awaitingCount) + \" awaiting an outcome\", link: \"Update outcomes\" } : null,\n      };\n    }\n    if (eligible.length && !pursued.length) {\n      return {\n        key: \"ready\", statusLabel: \"READY TO DISPUTE\", color: \"purple\",\n        supporting: \"No dispute has been started.\", primaryKind: \"prep\", primaryLabel: \"Prepare dispute for Biller\",\n      };\n    }\n    // no eligible remaining, everything has been sent\n    if (awaitingCount === 0) {\n      return {\n        key: \"completed\", statusLabel: \"DISPUTE COMPLETED\", color: \"green\",\n        supporting: pursued.length + \" of \" + pursued.length + \" findings finalized.\",\n        primaryKind: \"view\", primaryLabel: \"View dispute details\",\n      };\n    }\n    if (finalizedCount > 0) {\n      return {\n        key: \"partial\", statusLabel: \"OUTCOMES PARTIALLY RECORDED\", color: \"amber\",\n        supporting: finalizedCount + \" of \" + pursued.length + \" findings finalized.\",\n        primaryKind: \"outcome\", primaryLabel: \"Update dispute outcomes\",\n      };\n    }\n    return {\n      key: \"awaiting\", statusLabel: \"AWAITING BILLER RESPONSE\", color: \"amber\",\n      supporting: fmtCount(awaitingCount) + \" awaiting an outcome.\",\n      primaryKind: \"outcome\", primaryLabel: \"Update dispute outcomes\",\n    };\n  }\n  memoLevelCta() {\n    const md = this.state.memoDispute;\n    if (!md) {\n      return { key: \"ready\", statusLabel: \"READY TO DISPUTE\", color: \"purple\", supporting: \"No dispute has been started.\", primaryKind: \"prep\", primaryLabel: \"Prepare dispute for Biller\" };\n    }\n    if (md.status === \"awaiting\") {\n      return { key: \"awaiting\", statusLabel: \"AWAITING BILLER RESPONSE\", color: \"amber\", supporting: \"Awaiting a response from your biller.\", primaryKind: \"outcome\", primaryLabel: \"Update dispute outcomes\" };\n    }\n    return { key: \"completed\", statusLabel: \"DISPUTE COMPLETED\", color: \"green\", supporting: \"This dispute has been finalized.\", primaryKind: \"view\", primaryLabel: \"View dispute details\" };\n  }\n  memoOutcomeStatusLabel(status) { return { awaiting: \"Awaiting outcome\", full: \"Fully collected\", partial: \"Partly collected\", declined: \"Biller declined\" }[status] || \"Awaiting outcome\"; }\n  openMemoLevelOutcome = () => {\n    const md = this.state.memoDispute; if (!md) return;\n    this.setState({ memoOutcomeEdit: { status: md.status, amount: md.collectedAmountN != null ? String(md.collectedAmountN) : \"\", date: md.receivedDate || \"\", reason: md.reason || \"\", error: \"\" } });\n  };\n  closeMemoLevelOutcome = () => this.setState({ memoOutcomeEdit: null });\n  onMemoOutcomeStatus = (e) => { const v = e.currentTarget.dataset.v; this.setState(s => ({ memoOutcomeEdit: { ...s.memoOutcomeEdit, status: v, error: \"\" } })); };\n  onMemoOutcomeAmount = (e) => { const v = e.target.value; this.setState(s => ({ memoOutcomeEdit: { ...s.memoOutcomeEdit, amount: v } })); };\n  onMemoOutcomeDate = (e) => { const v = e.target.value; this.setState(s => ({ memoOutcomeEdit: { ...s.memoOutcomeEdit, date: v } })); };\n  saveMemoLevelOutcome = () => {\n    const d = this.state.memoOutcomeEdit; if (!d) return;\n    if (d.status === \"partial\" && (!d.amount || isNaN(parseFloat(d.amount)))) {\n      this.setState(s => ({ memoOutcomeEdit: { ...s.memoOutcomeEdit, error: \"Enter the amount collected.\" } })); return;\n    }\n    this.setState(s => ({\n      memoDispute: { ...s.memoDispute, status: d.status, collectedAmountN: d.status === \"full\" ? (s.memoDispute.amountN || 0) : (d.status === \"partial\" ? parseFloat(d.amount) : null), receivedDate: d.date || null, reason: d.status === \"declined\" ? d.reason : \"\" },\n      memoOutcomeEdit: null,\n    }), () => this.showToast(\"ok\", \"Outcome updated.\"));\n  };\n  openMemoLevelDetails = () => this.setState({ memoDetailsOpen: true });\n  closeMemoLevelDetails = () => this.setState({ memoDetailsOpen: false });\n  nextStepCard() {\n    const r = this._nextStepCardInner();\n    if (r) r.notResolved = !r.resolvedTreatment;\n    return r;\n  }\n  _nextStepCardInner() {\n    if (this.state.findingsUnavailable) {\n      const provider = (this.activeMemo() || {}).provider || \"Biller\";\n      const md = this.state.memoDispute;\n      if (!md) {\n        return {\n          action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"prep\", \"primary\", \"Prepare dispute for Biller\")], usePrep: true,\n          eyebrow: \"NEXT STEP\", heading: \"Prepare your dispute for \" + provider,\n          lines: [], description: \"A variance-group breakdown is not available for this credit memo, so the complete credit memo will be sent as a single dispute.\",\n        };\n      }\n      if (md.status === \"awaiting\") {\n        return {\n          action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"outcome\", \"primary\", \"Update dispute outcomes\")],\n          eyebrow: \"NEXT STEP\", heading: \"Track your dispute with \" + provider,\n          lines: [this.nsLine(\"clock\", \"Awaiting a response from your biller\")], description: \"\",\n        };\n      }\n      return {\n        action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"view\", \"primary\", \"View dispute details\")], resolvedTreatment: true,\n        eyebrow: \"RESOLVED\", heading: \"Your dispute with \" + provider + \" is complete\",\n        lines: [this.nsLine(\"check\", \"Dispute finalized\")], description: \"\",\n      };\n    }\n    const groups = this.state.reviewGroups || [];\n    if (!groups.length) return null;\n    const eligible = groups.filter(g => g.pursuit !== \"pursued\" && g.pursuit !== \"excluded\" && !this.groupExpired(g));\n    const pursued = groups.filter(g => g.pursuit === \"pursued\");\n    if (!eligible.length && !pursued.length) return null;\n    const provider = (this.activeMemo() || {}).provider || \"Biller\";\n    const fmtCount = (n) => n + \" finding\" + (n === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\");\n    const eligibleAmt = eligible.reduce((s, g) => s + (g.amountN || 0), 0);\n    // \"awaiting\" bucket for card messaging = raw awaiting findings + unresolved remainder of partial findings\n    let awaitingAmt = 0, awaitingCount = 0;\n    pursued.forEach(g => {\n      const st = g.collection && g.collection.status;\n      if (!st || st === \"awaiting\") { awaitingAmt += g.amountN || 0; awaitingCount++; }\n      else if (st === \"partial\") { const rem = Math.max(0, (g.amountN || 0) - (g.collection.amountN || 0)); if (rem > 0.005) { awaitingAmt += rem; awaitingCount++; } }\n    });\n    const prepLabel = eligible.length && pursued.length ? \"Prepare another dispute\" : \"Prepare dispute for Biller\";\n\n    if (pursued.length && eligible.length) {\n      // State 2: eligible + awaiting coexist\n      const actions = [this.nsAction(\"prep\", \"primary\", prepLabel)];\n      if (awaitingAmt > 0.005) actions.push(this.nsAction(\"outcome\", \"secondary\", \"Update dispute outcomes\"));\n      return {\n        action: true, actions, usePrep: true,\n        eyebrow: \"NEXT STEPS\", heading: \"Continue your work with \" + provider,\n        lines: [\n          this.nsLine(\"prep\", this.fmt(eligibleAmt) + \" across \" + fmtCount(eligible.length) + \" remains eligible for another dispute\"),\n          ...(awaitingAmt > 0.005 ? [this.nsLine(\"clock\", this.fmt(awaitingAmt) + \" across \" + fmtCount(awaitingCount) + \" is awaiting an outcome\")] : []),\n        ],\n        description: \"A new dispute for the remaining eligible findings will not affect any dispute already sent to \" + provider + \".\",\n      };\n    }\n    if (pursued.length && !eligible.length) {\n      const fullyResolved = awaitingAmt <= 0.005;\n      const collected = pursued.reduce((s, g) => s + ((g.collection && (g.collection.status === \"full\" || g.collection.status === \"partial\")) ? (g.collection.amountN || 0) : 0), 0);\n      if (fullyResolved) {\n        // State 4: everything finalized\n        return {\n          action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"view\", \"primary\", \"View previous dispute\")], resolvedTreatment: true,\n          eyebrow: \"RESOLVED\", heading: \"Your dispute with \" + provider + \" is complete\",\n          lines: [this.nsLine(\"check\", fmtCount(pursued.length) + \" finalized \\u00b7 \" + this.fmt(collected) + \" collected\")],\n          description: \"All findings have a final outcome.\",\n        };\n      }\n      // State 3: no eligible, outcomes still awaiting -> Update dispute outcomes promoted to primary\n      return {\n        action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"outcome\", \"primary\", \"Update dispute outcomes\")],\n        eyebrow: \"NEXT STEP\", heading: \"Track your dispute with \" + provider,\n        lines: [this.nsLine(\"clock\", this.fmt(awaitingAmt) + \" across \" + fmtCount(awaitingCount) + \" awaiting an outcome\")],\n        description: \"Record collection outcomes as your biller responds.\",\n      };\n    }\n    // only eligible groups, nothing pursued yet\n    const excludedIds = this.state.disputeExcludedIds || [];\n    const excludedCount = eligible.filter(g => excludedIds.includes(g.id)).length;\n    if (excludedCount > 0 && excludedCount < eligible.length) {\n      const selected = eligible.filter(g => !excludedIds.includes(g.id));\n      const selAmt = selected.reduce((s, g) => s + (g.amountN || 0), 0);\n      return {\n        action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"prep\", \"primary\", \"Continue preparing dispute\")], usePrep: true,\n        eyebrow: \"NEXT STEP\", heading: \"Continue preparing your dispute for \" + provider,\n        lines: [this.nsLine(\"prep\", this.fmt(selAmt) + \" selected across \" + fmtCount(selected.length))],\n        description: \"Your dispute draft has not been sent yet.\",\n      };\n    }\n    // State 1: nothing sent, all eligible\n    return {\n      action: true, actions: [this.nsAction(\"prep\", \"primary\", \"Prepare dispute for Biller\")], usePrep: true,\n      eyebrow: \"NEXT STEP\", heading: \"Prepare your dispute for \" + provider,\n      lines: [this.nsLine(\"prep\", this.fmt(eligibleAmt) + \" eligible across \" + fmtCount(eligible.length))],\n      description: \"Select the findings to include, then review and send your dispute package.\",\n    };\n  }\n  recoveryStatus() {\n    const groups = this.state.reviewGroups || [];\n    if (!groups.length) return null;\n    const total = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + (g.amountN || 0), 0);\n    if (!total) return null;\n    let collected = 0, declined = 0, awaiting = 0, eligible = 0;\n    groups.forEach(g => {\n      if (g.pursuit !== \"pursued\") { if (g.pursuit !== \"excluded\" && !this.groupExpired(g)) eligible += g.amountN || 0; return; }\n      const c = g.collection || {};\n      if (c.status === \"full\") collected += g.amountN || 0;\n      else if (c.status === \"not_issued\") declined += g.amountN || 0;\n      else if (c.status === \"partial\") { const amt = c.amountN || 0; collected += amt; awaiting += Math.max(0, (g.amountN || 0) - amt); }\n      else awaiting += g.amountN || 0;\n    });\n    const cats = [\n      { label: \"Collected\", color: \"var(--imp-success)\", amt: collected },\n      { label: \"Biller declined\", color: \"var(--imp-error)\", amt: declined },\n      { label: \"Awaiting outcome\", color: \"var(--imp-orange-500)\", amt: awaiting },\n      { label: \"Eligible to pursue\", color: \"var(--imp-purple-500)\", amt: eligible },\n    ];\n    const CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * 50;\n    let cum = 0;\n    const slices = cats.map(c => {\n      const dash = (c.amt / total) * CIRC;\n      const seg = { label: c.label, color: c.color, amount: this.fmt(c.amt), dashArray: dash.toFixed(2) + \" \" + Math.max(CIRC - dash, 0).toFixed(2), dashOffset: (-cum).toFixed(2) };\n      cum += dash;\n      return seg;\n    });\n    const resolved = awaiting <= 0.005 && eligible <= 0.005 && (collected + declined) > 0;\n    return { total: this.fmt(total), slices, eyebrow: resolved ? \"RESOLVED\" : \"RECOVERY STATUS\" };\n  }\n  memoDisputeStatus() {\n    const groups = (this.state.reviewGroups || []).filter(g => g.pursuit === \"pursued\");\n    if (!groups.length) return null;\n    const threePl = groups[0].threePl || (this.activeMemo() || {}).provider || \"Biller\";\n    const sentDate = groups[0].pursuedAt || \"\";\n    const amountPursued = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + (g.amountN || 0), 0);\n    const statuses = groups.map(g => (g.collection && g.collection.status) || \"awaiting\");\n    const allAwaiting = statuses.every(s => s === \"awaiting\");\n    const allFull = statuses.every(s => s === \"full\");\n    const allNotIssued = statuses.every(s => s === \"not_issued\");\n    let label = \"Awaiting outcome\";\n    if (allAwaiting) label = \"Awaiting outcome\";\n    else if (allFull) label = \"Fully collected\";\n    else if (allNotIssued) label = \"Biller declined\";\n    else label = \"Partially resolved\";\n    const step3Done = !allAwaiting;\n    return {\n      threePl, sentDate, amountPursued: this.fmt(amountPursued),\n      count: groups.length, label, step3Done,\n      step3Color: step3Done ? \"var(--imp-purple-500)\" : \"var(--imp-gray-300)\",\n      step3Bg: step3Done ? \"var(--imp-purple-500)\" : \"#fff\",\n      step3IconColor: step3Done ? \"#fff\" : \"var(--imp-gray-300)\",\n      step3LineColor: step3Done ? \"var(--imp-purple-500)\" : \"var(--imp-gray-300)\",\n    };\n  }\n  validateOmRow(g, d) {\n    if (d.status === \"partial\") {\n      const amt = parseFloat(d.amount || \"\");\n      const pursued = (g && g.amountN) || 0;\n      if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) return \"Enter a credit amount greater than $0.00.\";\n      if (amt > pursued + 0.005) return \"Credit received cannot exceed the amount pursued of \" + this.fmt(pursued) + \".\";\n    }\n    return \"\";\n  }\n  saveFinding = (e) => {\n    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    const om = this.state.outcomeModal; if (!om) return;\n    const g = (this.state.reviewGroups || []).find(x => x.id === id); if (!g) return;\n    let draft = om.drafts[id];\n    if (draft.status === \"partial\") {\n      const amt = parseFloat(draft.amount || \"\");\n      if (!isNaN(amt) && amt > 0 && Math.abs(amt - (g.amountN || 0)) < 0.005) draft = { ...draft, status: \"full\", amount: String(g.amountN || 0) };\n    }\n    const err = this.validateOmRow(g, draft);\n    if (err) { this.setState(s => ({ outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...draft, error: err } } } })); return; }\n    const now = this.nowStr();\n    const amt = parseFloat(draft.amount || \"0\") || 0;\n    const prior = (g.collection && g.collection.status && g.collection.status !== \"awaiting\") ? { status: g.collection.status, amountN: g.collection.amountN, date: g.collection.date, reason: g.collection.reason, changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now } : null;\n    const history = (g.collection && g.collection.history) ? g.collection.history.slice() : [];\n    if (prior) history.push(prior);\n    const next = { status: draft.status, amountN: draft.status === \"full\" ? (g.amountN || 0) : (draft.status === \"not_issued\" ? 0 : (draft.status === \"awaiting\" ? null : amt)), date: draft.status === \"awaiting\" ? null : (draft.date || null), reason: draft.status === \"not_issued\" ? draft.reason : \"\", changedBy: \"Tori Matthews\", changedAt: now, history };\n    this.trackEvent(\"collection_outcome_recorded\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", groupId: id, status: next.status, amount: next.amountN });\n    this.setState(s => ({\n      reviewGroups: s.reviewGroups.map(x => x.id === id ? { ...x, collection: next } : x),\n      outcomeModal: { ...s.outcomeModal, openFindingId: null, drafts: { ...s.outcomeModal.drafts, [id]: { ...draft, error: \"\" } } },\n    }));\n  };\n  onOcFilter = (e) => {\n    const k = e.currentTarget.dataset.key; const v = e.target.value;\n    this.setState({ [k]: v, ocOpenNotesId: null, ocDisposition: \"all\", ocSelectedSlice: null });\n  };\n  toggleFindingsPanel = (e) => { if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation(); this.setState(s => ({ fpOpen: s.fpOpen === \"findings\" ? null : \"findings\" })); };\n  clearFindingField = (e) => { const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key; this.setState({ [key]: \"all\" }); };\n  clearOcFilters = () => this.setState({ ocPeriod: \"all\", ocProvider: \"all\", ocGroup: \"all\", ocPursuit: \"all\", ocOutcome: \"all\", ocOpenNotesId: null, ocSelectedSlice: null, ocDisposition: \"all\" });\n  onSliceEnter = (e) => this.setState({ ocHoverSlice: e.currentTarget.dataset.key });\n  onSliceLeave = () => this.setState({ ocHoverSlice: null });\n  onSliceClick = (e) => {\n    if (e.currentTarget.blur) e.currentTarget.blur();\n    const k = e.currentTarget.dataset.key || e.currentTarget.dataset.v;\n    this.setState(s => s.ocDisposition === k ? { ocDisposition: \"all\", ocSelectedSlice: null } : { ocDisposition: k, ocSelectedSlice: k, ocPursuit: \"all\", ocOutcome: \"all\" });\n  };\n  toggleOcNotes = (e) => { const gid = e.currentTarget.dataset.gid; this.setState(s => ({ ocOpenNotesId: s.ocOpenNotesId === gid ? null : gid })); };\n  sliceCategory(g) {\n    if (g.pursuit === \"pursued\") { const s = (g.collection && g.collection.status) || \"awaiting\"; return (s === \"partial\" || s === \"full\" || s === \"not_issued\") ? s : \"awaiting\"; }\n    return \"eligible\";\n  }\n  dispositionAmounts(rows) {\n    let eligible = 0, awaiting = 0, collected = 0, denied = 0;\n    rows.forEach(g => {\n      const amt = g.amountN || 0;\n      if (g.pursuit !== \"pursued\") { eligible += amt; return; }\n      const c = g.collection || {};\n      if (c.status === \"full\") { collected += amt; return; }\n      if (c.status === \"not_issued\") { denied += amt; return; }\n      if (c.status === \"partial\") { const coll = c.amountN || 0; collected += coll; awaiting += Math.max(0, this.r2(amt - coll)); return; }\n      awaiting += amt;\n    });\n    return { eligible: this.r2(eligible), awaiting: this.r2(awaiting), collected: this.r2(collected), denied: this.r2(denied) };\n  }\n  segTopBorder(active, color) { return \"border-top:3px solid \" + (active ? color : \"transparent\") + \";\"; }\n  segNumColor(active, color) { return \"color:\" + color + \";\"; }\n  dispositionMatch(g, key) {\n    if (g.pursuit !== \"pursued\") return key === \"eligible\";\n    const c = g.collection || {};\n    if (key === \"denied\") return c.status === \"not_issued\";\n    if (key === \"collected\") return c.status === \"full\" || (c.status === \"partial\" && (c.amountN || 0) > 0.005);\n    if (key === \"awaiting\") { if (c.status === \"partial\") return this.r2((g.amountN || 0) - (c.amountN || 0)) > 0.005; return !c.status || c.status === \"awaiting\"; }\n    return false;\n  }\n  dispositionDonut(rows) {\n    const catDefs = [\n      { key: \"eligible\", label: \"Eligible to pursue\", color: \"var(--imp-purple-500)\" },\n      { key: \"awaiting\", label: \"Awaiting outcome\", color: \"var(--imp-orange-500)\" },\n      { key: \"collected\", label: \"Collected\", color: \"var(--imp-success)\" },\n      { key: \"denied\", label: \"Denied by Biller\", color: \"#B8756D\" },\n    ];\n    const amts = this.dispositionAmounts(rows);\n    const total = this.r2(amts.eligible + amts.awaiting + amts.collected + amts.denied);\n    const R = 68, CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * R, GAP = 3, MIN_DASH = 26, MAX_STROKE = 18;\n    const base = catDefs.map(cd => {\n      const amt = amts[cd.key];\n      const rawDash = total > 0 ? (amt / total) * CIRC : 0;\n      return { cd, amt, dash: amt > 0 ? Math.max(rawDash, MIN_DASH) : 0 };\n    });\n    const scale = total > 0 ? CIRC / base.reduce((a, b) => a + b.dash, 0) : 1;\n    let cum = 0;\n    const slices = base.map(({ cd, amt, dash: rawDash }) => {\n      const dash = rawDash * scale;\n      const pctN = total > 0 ? (amt / total * 100) : 0;\n      const gap = Math.min(GAP + MAX_STROKE, dash * 0.85);\n      const visDash = Math.max(dash - gap, 0);\n      const seg = {\n        key: cd.key, label: cd.label, color: cd.color,\n        amountN: amt, amount: this.fmt(amt), pct: Math.round(pctN) + \"%\",\n        dashArray: visDash.toFixed(2) + \" \" + Math.max(CIRC - visDash, 0).toFixed(2), dashOffset: (-cum).toFixed(2),\n      };\n      cum += dash;\n      return seg;\n    });\n    const slicesZ = slices.slice().sort((a, b) => b.amountN - a.amountN);\n    const slicesVisible = slices.filter(s => s.amountN > 0);\n    return { slices, slicesVisible, slicesZ, total: this.fmt(total), totalN: total, empty: total <= 0 || rows.length === 0, radius: R, circumference: CIRC };\n  }\n  outcomeDonut(rows) {\n    const catDefs = [\n      { key: \"eligible\", label: \"Eligible to pursue\", color: \"var(--imp-purple-500)\" },\n      { key: \"awaiting\", label: \"Awaiting outcome\", color: \"var(--imp-orange-500)\" },\n      { key: \"partial\", label: \"Partly collected\", color: \"#1F9E86\" },\n      { key: \"full\", label: \"Fully collected\", color: \"var(--imp-success)\" },\n      { key: \"not_issued\", label: \"Credit not issued\", color: \"#B8756D\" },\n    ];\n    const total = this.r2(rows.reduce((a, g) => a + (g.amountN || 0), 0));\n    const R = 68, CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * R, GAP = 3, MIN_DASH = 26, MAX_STROKE = 18;\n    const base = catDefs.map(cd => {\n      const grp = rows.filter(g => this.sliceCategory(g) === cd.key);\n      const amt = this.r2(grp.reduce((a, g) => a + (g.amountN || 0), 0));\n      const rawDash = total > 0 ? (amt / total) * CIRC : 0;\n      return { cd, grp, amt, dash: amt > 0 ? Math.max(rawDash, MIN_DASH) : 0 };\n    });\n    const scale = total > 0 ? CIRC / base.reduce((a, b) => a + b.dash, 0) : 1;\n    let cum = 0;\n    const slices = base.map(({ cd, grp, amt, dash: rawDash }) => {\n      const dash = rawDash * scale;\n      const collectedAmt = this.r2(grp.reduce((a, g) => a + ((g.collection && g.collection.amountN) || 0), 0));\n      const pctN = total > 0 ? (amt / total * 100) : 0;\n      const gap = Math.min(GAP + MAX_STROKE, dash * 0.85);\n      const visDash = Math.max(dash - gap, 0);\n      const seg = {\n        key: cd.key, label: cd.label, color: cd.color, count: grp.length,\n        countLabel: grp.length + (grp.length === 1 ? \" group\" : \" groups\"),\n        amountN: amt, amount: this.fmt(amt), pct: Math.round(pctN) + \"%\",\n        dashArray: visDash.toFixed(2) + \" \" + Math.max(CIRC - visDash, 0).toFixed(2), dashOffset: (-cum).toFixed(2),\n        pursuedAmount: cd.key === \"eligible\" ? null : this.fmt(amt),\n        collectedAmount: (cd.key === \"partial\" || cd.key === \"full\") ? this.fmt(collectedAmt) : null,\n        unresolvedAmount: cd.key === \"partial\" ? this.fmt(Math.max(0, amt - collectedAmt)) : null,\n        hasBreakdown: cd.key === \"partial\" || cd.key === \"full\",\n      };\n      cum += dash;\n      return seg;\n    });\n    const slicesZ = slices.slice().sort((a, b) => b.amountN - a.amountN);\n    const slicesVisible = slices.filter(s => s.amountN > 0);\n    return { slices, slicesVisible, slicesZ, total: this.fmt(total), totalN: total, empty: total <= 0 || rows.length === 0, radius: R, circumference: CIRC };\n  }\n\n  // ---------- report-level disputes (FCM / PWV / LCC) ----------\n  reportById(kind, id) { const list = kind === \"fcm\" ? this.fcmData() : kind === \"pwv\" ? this.pwvData() : this.lccData(); return (list || []).find(r => r.id === id); }\n  reportMeta(kind, rep) {\n    if (kind === \"fcm\") return { biller: rep.provider || \"Implentio\", amountN: rep.overbilledN, volumeLabel: rep.orders.toLocaleString(\"en-US\") + \" orders reviewed\", scope: \"the available fulfillment billing data\", oppWord: \"overbilling\", oppPhrase: \"in potential overbilling\", subjectPrefix: \"Implentio fulfillment billing review\", kindLabel: \"Fulfillment credit memo\", evidenceUnit: \"order-level\", identityLabel: \"Fulfillment report\", zipPrefix: \"Fulfillment\", summaryNoun: \"fulfillment report\", disputeModalTitle: \"Update fulfillment dispute outcome\", disputeRecordLabel: \"Fulfillment report dispute\" };\n    if (kind === \"pwv\") return { biller: \"Implentio\", amountN: rep.overbillingN, volumeLabel: rep.packages.toLocaleString(\"en-US\") + \" packages analyzed\", scope: \"product and package weight billing data\", oppWord: \"weight-related overbilling\", oppPhrase: \"in potential weight-related overbilling\", subjectPrefix: \"Implentio product weight billing review\", kindLabel: \"Product weight validation analysis\", evidenceUnit: \"package-level\", identityLabel: \"Product Weight Validator report\", zipPrefix: \"PWV\", summaryNoun: \"Product Weight Validator report\", disputeModalTitle: \"Update Product Weight Validator dispute outcome\", disputeRecordLabel: \"Product Weight Validator report dispute\" };\n    return { biller: \"Implentio\", amountN: rep.savingsN, volumeLabel: rep.packages.toLocaleString(\"en-US\") + \" packages analyzed\", scope: \"carrier and service-level data\", oppWord: \"savings opportunities\", oppPhrase: \"in potential savings opportunities\", subjectPrefix: \"Implentio carrier cost review\", kindLabel: \"Least cost carrier analysis\", evidenceUnit: \"shipment-level\", identityLabel: \"Least Cost Carrier report\", zipPrefix: \"LCC\", summaryNoun: \"Least Cost Carrier report\", disputeModalTitle: \"Update Least Cost Carrier dispute outcome\", disputeRecordLabel: \"Least Cost Carrier report dispute\" };\n  }\n  rdKey(kind, id) { return kind + \":\" + id; }\n  rdGet(kind, id) { return (this.state.reportDisputes || {})[this.rdKey(kind, id)] || null; }\n  reportOutcomeLabel(status) { return { awaiting: \"Awaiting outcome\", full: \"Fully collected\", partial: \"Partly collected\", denied: \"Denied by Biller\" }[status] || \"Awaiting outcome\"; }\n  reportLifecycleCta(kind, rep) {\n    const rd = this.rdGet(kind, rep.id);\n    if (!rd) return { key: \"ready\", statusLabel: \"READY TO DISPUTE\", color: \"purple\", supporting: \"No dispute has been started.\", primaryKind: \"prep\", primaryLabel: \"Prepare dispute for Biller\" };\n    if (rd.status === \"draft\") return { key: \"draft\", statusLabel: \"DISPUTE DRAFT\", color: \"purple\", supporting: \"Started \" + rd.draftDate + \" \\u00b7 Not yet sent.\", primaryKind: \"prep\", primaryLabel: \"Continue preparing dispute\" };\n    if (rd.status === \"awaiting\") return { key: \"awaiting\", statusLabel: \"AWAITING BILLER RESPONSE\", color: \"amber\", supporting: \"Outcome has not been recorded.\", primaryKind: \"outcome\", primaryLabel: \"Update dispute outcome\" };\n    if (rd.status === \"partial\") return { key: \"partial\", statusLabel: \"OUTCOME PARTIALLY RECORDED\", color: \"amber\", supporting: this.fmt(rd.collectedAmountN || 0) + \" collected \\u00b7 \" + this.fmt(Math.max(0, (rd.amountN || 0) - (rd.collectedAmountN || 0))) + \" unresolved\", primaryKind: \"outcome\", primaryLabel: \"Update dispute outcome\" };\n    return { key: \"done\", statusLabel: \"DISPUTE COMPLETED\", color: rd.status === \"denied\" ? \"red\" : \"green\", supporting: \"Final outcome recorded.\", primaryKind: \"view\", primaryLabel: \"View dispute details\" };\n  }\n  openReportPrep = (e) => {\n    const kind = e.currentTarget.dataset.kind; const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id;\n    const rep = this.reportById(kind, id); if (!rep) return;\n    const meta = this.reportMeta(kind, rep);\n    this.trackEvent(\"prepare_report_dispute_started\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", kind, reportId: id });\n    const rd = this.rdGet(kind, id);\n    if (rd && rd.status === \"draft\") {\n      this.setState({ prepReportKind: kind, prepReportId: id, prepStep: 2, activeNote: null, prepSendPhase: \"idle\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    this.setState({\n      prepReportKind: kind, prepReportId: id, prepStep: 1, activeNote: null,\n      prepAttachPdf: true, prepAttachXlsx: true,\n      prepTo: \"\", prepCc: \"\",\n      prepSubject: meta.subjectPrefix + \" \\u2014 \" + rep.period + \" \\u2014 \" + rep.id,\n      prepIntro: \"Implentio reviewed \" + meta.scope + \" for \" + rep.period + \" and identified \" + this.fmt(meta.amountN || 0) + \" \" + meta.oppPhrase + \".\",\n      prepSendPhase: \"idle\", prepManualChosen: false, prepCsmOptIn: false, prepManualActionTaken: false,\n    });\n  };\n  reportPrepGoConnect = () => this.setState({ prepManualChosen: false, prepStep: 2 });\n  deferReportPrep = () => {\n    const st = this.state; const kind = st.prepReportKind; const id = st.prepReportId; if (!kind || !id) { this.closeReportPrep(); return; }\n    const key = this.rdKey(kind, id);\n    const attachments = [st.prepAttachPdf ? \"Summary report (PDF)\" : null, st.prepAttachXlsx ? \"Supporting evidence (Excel)\" : null].filter(Boolean);\n    this.trackEvent(\"report_dispute_draft_saved\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", kind, reportId: id });\n    this.setState(s => ({\n      reportDisputes: { ...(s.reportDisputes || {}), [key]: { status: \"draft\", draftDate: this.nowStr(), amountN: this.reportMeta(kind, this.reportById(kind, id)).amountN || 0, attachments, collectedAmountN: null, receivedDate: null, reason: \"\" } },\n      prepReportKind: null, prepReportId: null,\n    }));\n  };\n  closeReportPrep = () => this.setState({ prepReportKind: null, prepReportId: null });\n  reportPrepBack = () => this.setState(s => (s.prepStep || 1) <= 1 ? { prepReportKind: null, prepReportId: null } : { prepStep: s.prepStep - 1 });\n  reportPrepNext = () => this.setState(s => ({ prepStep: Math.min(3, (s.prepStep || 1) + 1) }));\n  toggleReportAttachPdf = () => this.setState(s => ({ prepAttachPdf: !s.prepAttachPdf }));\n  toggleReportAttachXlsx = () => this.setState(s => ({ prepAttachXlsx: !s.prepAttachXlsx }));\n  selectAllReportAttach = () => this.setState({ prepAttachPdf: true, prepAttachXlsx: true });\n  clearReportAttach = () => this.setState({ prepAttachPdf: false, prepAttachXlsx: false });\n  onPrepTo = (e) => this.setState({ prepTo: e.target.value });\n  onPrepCc = (e) => this.setState({ prepCc: e.target.value });\n  onPrepSubject = (e) => this.setState({ prepSubject: e.target.value });\n  onPrepIntro = (e) => this.setState({ prepIntro: e.target.value });\n  sendReportPrepared = () => {\n    const st = this.state; const kind = st.prepReportKind; const id = st.prepReportId;\n    const rep = this.reportById(kind, id); if (!rep) return;\n    if (st.prepSendPhase === \"processing\") return;\n    this.setState({ prepSendPhase: \"processing\" });\n    clearTimeout(this._sendTimer);\n    this._sendTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      const acct = this.prepEmailAccount();\n      const meta = this.reportMeta(kind, rep);\n      const now = this.nowStr();\n      const key = this.rdKey(kind, id);\n      const attachments = [st.prepAttachPdf ? \"Summary report (PDF)\" : null, st.prepAttachXlsx ? \"Supporting evidence (Excel)\" : null].filter(Boolean);\n      const via = acct ? \"connected\" : \"manual\";\n      this.trackEvent(\"report_dispute_submitted\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", kind, reportId: id, provider: acct ? acct.provider : \"manual\", attachments, via });\n      this.setState(s => ({\n        prepSendPhase: \"sent\", prepSentAt: new Date().toLocaleString(\"en-US\"), prepSentEmail: acct ? acct.email : \"tori@implentio.com\", prepSentCc: s.prepCc || \"\",\n        reportDisputes: { ...(s.reportDisputes || {}), [key]: { status: \"awaiting\", sentAt: now, amountN: meta.amountN || 0, attachments, collectedAmountN: null, receivedDate: null, reason: \"\", to: s.prepTo || \"\", cc: s.prepCc || \"\" } },\n        activity: [{ icon: \"gen\", text: \"Dispute submitted to \" + meta.biller + \" \\u2014 \" + rep.name, time: this.nowStr() }, ...s.activity],\n      }));\n    }, 1500);\n  };\n  reportZipFilename(kind, rep, meta) {\n    const slug = (rep.periodKey || rep.period || \"\").trim().replace(/\\s+/g, \"-\");\n    return slug + \"-\" + meta.zipPrefix + \"-Selected-Evidence.zip\";\n  }\n  copyReportPreparedEmail = () => {\n    const st = this.state; const kind = st.prepReportKind; const rep = this.reportById(kind, st.prepReportId); if (!rep) return;\n    const meta = this.reportMeta(kind, rep);\n    const locked = \"Implentio reviewed \" + meta.scope + \" for \" + rep.period + \" and identified \" + this.fmt(meta.amountN || 0) + \" \" + meta.oppPhrase + \".\\n\\nThe attached report summarizes the findings, and the supporting evidence contains the available \" + meta.evidenceUnit + \" records and calculations.\\n\\nPlease review the findings and confirm what adjustment or next action will be taken.\";\n    const attachNames = [st.prepAttachPdf ? (rep.id + \"-summary.pdf\") : null, st.prepAttachXlsx ? (rep.id + \"-evidence.xlsx\") : null].filter(Boolean);\n    const text = \"To: \" + (st.prepTo || \"\") + \"\\nCC: \" + (st.prepCc || \"\") + \"\\nSubject: \" + (st.prepSubject || \"\") + \"\\nAttachment: \" + attachNames.join(\", \") + \"\\n\\n\" + (st.prepIntro || \"\") + \"\\n\\n\" + locked + \"\\n\\n\\u2014 Sent via Implentio\";\n    const done = () => { this.trackEvent(\"prepared_email_copied\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", kind, reportId: rep.id }); this.showToast(\"ok\", \"Email copied. Paste it into your email account and attach the evidence file.\"); };\n    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done).catch(() => this.showToast(\"warn\", \"The email could not be copied. Select the text and copy it manually.\"));\n    else this.showToast(\"warn\", \"The email could not be copied. Select the text and copy it manually.\");\n  };\n  downloadReportPreparedEvidence = () => {\n    const st = this.state; const kind = st.prepReportKind; const rep = this.reportById(kind, st.prepReportId); if (!rep) return;\n    const meta = this.reportMeta(kind, rep);\n    const name = this.reportZipFilename(kind, rep, meta);\n    const acct = this.prepEmailAccount();\n    this.trackEvent(\"selected_evidence_package_downloaded\", { account: \"Implentio\", user: \"tori@implentio.com\", kind, reportId: rep.id, pdf: !!st.prepAttachPdf, xlsx: !!st.prepAttachXlsx });\n    this.setState({ prepManualActionTaken: acct ? st.prepManualActionTaken : true }, () => this.showToast(\"ok\", \"Downloading \" + name));\n  };\n  dismissReportManualSent = () => this.setState({ prepManualActionTaken: false });\n  reportPrepVals() {\n    const st = this.state;\n    if (!st.prepReportKind) return { open: false };\n    const kind = st.prepReportKind; const rep = this.reportById(kind, st.prepReportId);\n    if (!rep) return { open: false };\n    const meta = this.reportMeta(kind, rep);\n    const step = st.prepStep || 1;\n    const acct = this.prepEmailAccount();\n    const attachCount = (st.prepAttachPdf ? 1 : 0) + (st.prepAttachXlsx ? 1 : 0);\n    const noSelection = attachCount === 0;\n    const labels = [\"Choose attachments\", \"Prepare email\", \"Ready to send\"];\n    const attachNames = [st.prepAttachPdf ? (rep.id + \"-summary.pdf\") : null, st.prepAttachXlsx ? recordMemoDisputeEvent(m, via) {\n    const now = new Date();\n    const when = now.toLocaleString(\"en-US\", { month: \"short\", day: \"numeric\", year: \"numeric\" }) + \" at \" + via, threePl: m.provider, memoId: m.id, version: m.version,\n      groupCount: 0, amount: this.fmt(m.netN || 0),\n      groups: [{ title: \"Complete credit memo\", amount: this.fmt(m.netN || 0) }],\n
